The History of Italy, Vol. 10 (Francesco Guicciardini (Austin Parke Goddard, trans.), 1561)
==========================================================
Guicciardini's Storia d'Italia (written 1537-40, first printed 1561) - the first 'modern' analytical history; the Italian wars as the tragedy of a divided peninsula. Goddard's English translation (1753-61, 10 vols.). Vol. 10 of 10.

A. D. rendered it very difficult for him to make

Wherefore he bent all his Thoughts and
Preparations on a Siege, wholly intent on

Venetian Gallies, in order to ſtop their
Importation by Sea. Then, changing his

In this State of Affairs the Imperialiſts
conceived Hopes of defeating Filipino

much Streſs on the Number and Goodneſs

Four Pinnaces, and Two Brigantines with
a Thouſand Spaniſh Harquebuſiers of the
moſt valiant and moſt reputable Troops in

Ugo the 'Viceroy; and almoſt all the Ge-
nerals and Men of Authority, To this

Gobbo, a famous Commander, and of long

ceſſors, no Thought of perpetuating the
Majeſty of the Pontificate, but, inſtead of
this, an ambitious and plaguy Defire of ex-

alting their Sons, Nephews, and Relati-

ons, not only to immoderate Wealth,
but to Principalities, to Kingdoms, no
longer diſtributing Dignities and Emolu-
ments to theWell-deſerving and Virtuous,
but almoſt conſtantly either ſelling them
at the deareſt Rate, or laviſhing them on
Perſons adminiſtering to their Ambition,
Avarice, or ſhameful Pleaſures.

fical Reverence being utterly extin-
